local humanoid342 = "a"
local EMPTY_ANIM_ID = "rbxassetid://14959617469"
local ANIM_PRIORITY = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
local function startEmptyAnimation()
	local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
	anim.AnimationId = EMPTY_ANIM_ID
	if readfile("ezka/dis_self_rep.txt") == "false" then
		local animTrack = humanoid342:LoadAnimation(anim)
		animTrack.Priority = ANIM_PRIORITY
		animTrack.Looped = true
		animTrack:Play()
	end
end
game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local character324 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	if not character324 then return end
	local humanoided = character324:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoided then return end
	if not humanoid342 then return end
	humanoid342 = humanoided
	local isAnimPlaying2 = false
	local playingTracks = humanoid342:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	for _, track in ipairs(playingTracks) do
		if track.Animation.AnimationId == EMPTY_ANIM_ID then
			isAnimPlaying2 = true
			break
		end
	end
	if not isAnimPlaying2 then
		startEmptyAnimation()
	end
end)

local p_d_l = {}
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TARGET_EMPTY_ANIM_ID = "rbxassetid://14959617469"
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local character = player.Character
		if character then
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid.Animator then
				local playingTracks = humanoid.Animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
				for _, track in ipairs(playingTracks) do
					local currentAnimId = track.Animation.AnimationId
					if currentAnimId == TARGET_EMPTY_ANIM_ID then
						if not p_d_l[player.Name] then
							p_d_l[player.Name] = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

	local function PU_EsH8Y()
		if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "" then return end
		print("Console Signal "..WIlJ6TZt.Text)
		if WIlJ6TZt.Text and string.sub(WIlJ6TZt.Text, 1, 1) == "/" then
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/removegui" then
				local dwdl12d18 = game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("EZKA HUB+")
				if dwdl12d18 then
					dwdl12d18:Destroy()
				end
				local dwdl12d18 = game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("Notification")
				if dwdl12d18 then
					dwdl12d18:Destroy()
				end
				local dwdl12d18 = game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("ESP")
				if dwdl12d18 then
					dwdl12d18:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/allon" then
				for index, _ in ipairs(values) do
					values[index] = true
				end
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/alloff" then
				for index, _ in ipairs(values) do
					values[index] = false
				end
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/vip" then
				vip_version = true
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/unvip" then
				vip_version = false
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/mobile" then
				mobile = true
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/unmobile" then
				mobile = false
			end
			if WIlJ6TZt.Text == "/disablesliderlimit" then
				gui_s1_sl = true	
			end
		else
			local success, chunk = pcall(loadstring, WIlJ6TZt.Text)
			if success then
				local successExec, err = pcall(chunk)

				if successExec then
				else
					warn("EZKA HUB DEV CONSOLE ERROR: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			else
				warn("EZKA HUB DEV CONSOLE ERROR: " .. tostring(chunk))
			end
		end
	end

		end
	end)

	end)

local function spirit_start()
	frame:Destroy()
	local a1239 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("StatusUI"):WaitForChild("IntroFrame")
	if support_fire_click_detector == false then
		lockMouse()

		local cccccccccd = false
		function fireclickdetector(d)
			if cccccccccd == false then
				cccccccccd = true
				local targetObject = nil

				if d.Parent:IsA("Model") then
					if d.Parent.PrimaryPart then
						targetObject = d.Parent.PrimaryPart
					else

						for _, child in ipairs(d.Parent:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("BasePart") then
								targetObject = child
								break
							end
						end
					end
				elseif d.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
					targetObject = d.Parent
				end

				if not targetObject then
					cccccccccd = false
					warn("fireclickdetector: error 1")
					return
				end

				local targetPosition3D = targetObject.Position
				local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera


				local cameraPosition = camera.CFrame.Position
				local cameraCFrame = camera.CFrame


				local directionToTarget = (targetPosition3D - cameraPosition).Unit


				local newCameraCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cameraPosition, targetPosition3D)

				camera.CFrame = newCameraCFrame

				task.wait(0.1) 

				mouse1click()
				task.wait(0.05)
				cccccccccd = false
			end
		end
	end
	local title2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title2.Parent = gui
	title2.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0.1, 0)

	local IS_MONSTER = false
	local monsterFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Monster")
	local previousProgress = {}
	local activeTrigger = {}
	local pendingChecks = {}
	local function check_monster()
		local anyActiveTrigger = false
		for _, model in pairs(monsterFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if not model:IsA("Model") or model.Name == "Closet" then
				continue
			end
			local progressValue = model:FindFirstChild("Progress")
			if not (progressValue and progressValue:IsA("NumberValue")) then
				continue
			end
			local current = progressValue.Value
			local prev = previousProgress[model.Name] or 0
			previousProgress[model.Name] = current
			if pendingChecks[model.Name] then
				continue
			end
			if activeTrigger[model.Name] then
				if current ~= 3 then
					activeTrigger[model.Name] = nil
				else
					anyActiveTrigger = true
				end
			else
				if prev == 2 and current == 3 then
					pendingChecks[model.Name] = true
					task.spawn(function()
						task.wait(0.2)
						local modelCheck = monsterFolder:FindFirstChild(model.Name)
						if not modelCheck then
							pendingChecks[model.Name] = nil
							return
						end
						local progressCheck = modelCheck:FindFirstChild("Progress")
						local now = (progressCheck and progressCheck:IsA("NumberValue")) and progressCheck.Value or -1
						if now == 3 then
							activeTrigger[model.Name] = true
							anyActiveTrigger = true
						else
							activeTrigger[model.Name] = nil
						end
						pendingChecks[model.Name] = nil
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		IS_MONSTER = anyActiveTrigger
	end


	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if a1239.Visible == false then
			if game.ReplicatedStorage.End.Value == false then
				check_monster()
				if IS_MONSTER == false and workspace.Bed.Hidden.Value == false then
					local Lamp = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lamp")
					local Heat = Lamp:FindFirstChild("Heat").Value
					if Heat < 40 and Lamp.Bulb.PointLight.Enabled == false then
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(8.28003407, 5.00000334, 18.7269535, -0.999789655, 7.96230548e-09, -0.0205098726, 7.06426828e-09, 1, 4.38580692e-08, 0.0205098726, 4.3703956e-08, -0.999789655)
						fireclickdetector(Lamp.ClickDetector)
					elseif Heat >= 70 and Lamp.Bulb.PointLight.Enabled == true then
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(8.28003407, 5.00000334, 18.7269535, -0.999789655, 7.96230548e-09, -0.0205098726, 7.06426828e-09, 1, 4.38580692e-08, 0.0205098726, 4.3703956e-08, -0.999789655)
						fireclickdetector(Lamp.ClickDetector)
					else
						local Teddy_bear = workspace:FindFirstChild("Teddy bear")
						if Teddy_bear.ClickDetector.MaxActivationDistance > 0 then
							game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(-8.32593918, 5.00000334, -9.43118858, 0.999313593, 2.59117634e-08, 0.0370455198, -2.71939431e-08, 1, 3.41070141e-08, -0.0370455198, -3.50910163e-08, 0.999313593)
							fireclickdetector(Teddy_bear.ClickDetector)
						else
							local Radio = workspace:FindFirstChild("Radio")
							if Radio.ClickDetector.MaxActivationDistance > 0 then
								game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(-10.4044113, 5.00000334, 16.6453609, -0.0674342215, -7.93165071e-08, 0.997723699, 1.08398401e-08, 1, 8.02301088e-08, -0.997723699, 1.6225421e-08, -0.0674342215)
								fireclickdetector(Radio.ClickDetector)
							else
								game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(9.89709663, 5.00000334, 18.5351982, 0.00391950598, -1.26934052e-09, 0.999992311, 1.08748999e-07, 1, 8.43104642e-10, -0.999992311, 1.08744857e-07, 0.00391950598)
							end
						end
					end
				elseif IS_MONSTER == false and workspace.Bed.Hidden.Value == true then
					fireclickdetector(workspace.Bed.Detectors.Detector2.ClickDetector)
				elseif IS_MONSTER == true and workspace.Bed.Hidden.Value == false then
					local Lamp = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lamp")
					if Lamp.Bulb.PointLight.Enabled == true then
						fireclickdetector(Lamp.ClickDetector)		
					end
					if Lamp.Bulb.PointLight.Enabled == false then
						fireclickdetector(workspace.Bed.Detectors.Detector2.ClickDetector)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function f_start()
	frame:Destroy() 
	
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Parent = gui

		local function gui1czc()
			local drframe = Instance.new("Frame")

			drframe.ZIndex = 100
			local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
			local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
			local dragging = false
			local dragStart = nil
			local startPos = nil

			local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			drframe.Active = true
			drframe.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
					dragging = true
					dragStart = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
					startPos = frame.Position
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)

				if not frame.Visible then
					dragging = false
					return
				end
				if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
					local currentMousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
					local delta = currentMousePos - dragStart

					local goalPos = UDim2.new(
						startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
						startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
					)
					local tween = TweenService:Create(frame, tweenInfo, {Position = goalPos})
					tween:Play()
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
			frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
				if not frame.Visible then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
		end
		gui1czc()
		local function gui2czc()
			local drframe = Instance.new("Frame")
			drframe.Parent = frame
			drframe.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.02, 0)
			drframe.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1.03, 0)
			drframe.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			drframe.Transparency = 0.2
			drframe.BorderSizePixel = 0
			drframe.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
			drframe.ZIndex = 100
			local uic = Instance.new("UICorner")
			uic.Parent = drframe
			uic.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
			local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
			local dragging = false
			local dragStart = nil
			local startPos = nil

			local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			drframe.Active = true
			drframe.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
					dragging = true
					dragStart = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
					startPos = frame.Position
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)

				if not frame.Visible then
					dragging = false
					return
				end
				if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
					local currentMousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
					local delta = currentMousePos - dragStart

					local goalPos = UDim2.new(
						startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
						startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
					)
					local tween = TweenService:Create(frame, tweenInfo, {Position = goalPos})
					tween:Play()
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
			frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
				if not frame.Visible then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
		end
		gui2czc()
	end

	local background_blur_background = Instance.new("Frame")

	local STAR_ID = "rbxassetid://96623230893691"
	local STARS_SPEED = 200
	local SPAWN_RARE_MULT = 1
	task.spawn(function()
		STARS_SPEED = 2000
		SPAWN_RARE_MULT = 2
		task.wait(2)
		STARS_SPEED = 200
		SPAWN_RARE_MULT = 1
	end)
	local BASE_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 0.75
	local BASE_WIDTH = 500
	local WIDTH_INFLUENCE = 1

	local IGNORE_VISIBILITY_TIME = 2
	local startTime = tick()
	
	local stars = {}
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

	local function getSpawnInterval()
		local width = background_blur_background.AbsoluteSize.X
		local ratio = BASE_WIDTH / math.max(width, 1)
		local factor = 1 + WIDTH_INFLUENCE * (ratio - 1)
		local interval = BASE_SPAWN_INTERVAL * factor
		return interval * SPAWN_RARE_MULT
	end


	local function spawnStar()
		local star = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		star.Name = "FallingStar"
		star.Image = STAR_ID
		star.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		star.ImageTransparency = background_blur_background.BackgroundTransparency

		local t = math.random() ^ 2
		local size = 35 + math.floor(25 * t + 0.5)
		star.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
		star.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

		star.Position = UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, 0, -size)
		star:SetAttribute("yOffset", -size)
		star:SetAttribute("size", size)
		star.Parent = background_blur_background
		table.insert(stars, star)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			if tick() - startTime < IGNORE_VISIBILITY_TIME or background_blur_background.Visible then
				spawnStar()
			end
			task.wait(getSpawnInterval())
		end
	end)

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if tick() - startTime >= IGNORE_VISIBILITY_TIME and not background_blur_background.Visible then
			return
		end
		local frameHeight = background_blur_background.AbsoluteSize.Y + 60

		for i = #stars, 1, -1 do
			local star = stars[i]
			if not star or not star.Parent then
				table.remove(stars, i)
				continue
			end

			local size = star:GetAttribute("size")
			local speed = STARS_SPEED * (35 / size)

			local yOffset = star:GetAttribute("yOffset") + speed * dt
			star:SetAttribute("yOffset", yOffset)

			local posX = star.Position.X
			star.Position = UDim2.new(posX.Scale, posX.Offset, 0, yOffset)
			star.ImageTransparency = background_blur_background.BackgroundTransparency

			if yOffset > frameHeight then
				star:Destroy()
				table.remove(stars, i)
			end
		end
	end)

	function new_notification(message,emoji,notification_color)
		if not emoji then
			emoji = "✅"
		end
		if not notification_color then
			notification_color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		end
		if can_notify == true and values["notif"] == true then
			local notif_s = Instance.new("Sound")
			notif_s.Parent = notification_gui
			notif_s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://117649901456711"
			notif_s.Playing = true
			notif_s.Played:Connect(function()
				notif_s:Destroy()
			end)
			local notification_item = Instance.new("Frame")
			notification_item.Parent = notification
			notification_item.BackgroundColor3 = color5
			notification_item.Transparency = 1
			notification_item.Size = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 1, 0)
			notification_item.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
			notification_item.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			local UIStroke2324 = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			UIStroke2324.Parent = notification_item
			UIStroke2324.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			UIStroke2324.Thickness = 0
			local notif_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			notif_text.Parent = notification_item
			notif_text.Text = message
			notif_text.TextScaled = true
			notif_text.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			notif_text.TextTransparency = 1
			notif_text.TextColor3 = notification_color
			notif_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
			notif_text.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
			notif_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 1, 0)
			notif_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			notif_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			notif_text.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

			local emoji_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			emoji_text.Parent = notification_item
			emoji_text.Text = emoji
			emoji_text.TextScaled = true
			emoji_text.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			emoji_text.TextTransparency = 1
			emoji_text.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			emoji_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			emoji_text.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
			emoji_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.085, 0, 1, 0)
			emoji_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			emoji_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			emoji_text.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
			Instance.new("UICorner", notification_item,0.1)

			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(notification_item, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Transparency = 0.3 }):Play()
			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(notif_text, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(emoji_text, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(UIStroke2324, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Thickness = 2 }):Play()
			local children = notification:GetChildren()
			local count = #children
			if count > 2 then

			end
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(5)

	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessedEvent)
		if gameProcessedEvent then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode.Name == Open_Gui_keybind_name then
			if Keybinds_frame.Visible == false then
				if can_open_g_fr == true then
					if enabled == false then
						unlockMouse()
						if mobile == true then
							cool_gui_background = true
						end
						enabled = true
						can_open_g_fr = false
						frame.Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
						game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = st_frame_size}):Play()
						wait(0.5)
						can_open_g_fr = true
					else
						lockMouse()
						cool_gui_background = false
						can_open_g_fr = false
						game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)}):Play()
						wait(0.5)
						can_open_g_fr = true
						enabled = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local function u_1oak_1(t)
		local StartContainer = t
		for _, stroke in ipairs(StartContainer:GetDescendants()) do	
			if stroke:IsA("UIStroke") or stroke:IsA("BoolValue") then
				if stroke:IsA("UIStroke") then
					local gdajb = stroke:FindFirstChild("UIGradient")
					if not gdajb then
						local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
						gradient.Parent = stroke
						gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color1),
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color2),
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color3)
						})
					else
						local rota = gdajb.Rotation
						local rv2 = rota + 359
						game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(gdajb, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Rotation = rv2}):Play()
					end
				else
					local gdajb = stroke.Parent:FindFirstChild("UIGradient")
					if not gdajb then

						local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
						gradient.Parent = stroke.Parent
						gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color1),
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color2),
							ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color3)
						})
					else
						local rota = gdajb.Rotation
						local rv2 = rota + 359
						game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(gdajb, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Rotation = rv2}):Play()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local can_update_pandfps = true
	local frameCount = 0
	local lastTime = tick()
	local currentPing = 0
	local function updateLabels()
		if can_update_pandfps == true then
			can_update_pandfps = false
			local currentPing2 = player:GetNetworkPing()
			local currentPing1 = currentPing2 * 1000
			currentPing = math.floor(currentPing1)
			ping.Text = string.format("%dms", currentPing)
			frameCount = frameCount + 1
			local now = tick()
			if now - lastTime >= 1 then
				fps.Text = string.format("%dfps", frameCount)
				frameCount = 0
				lastTime = now
			end
			can_update_pandfps = true
		end
	end

	game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		updateLabels()
	end)

	local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")

	local can_da = false
	local function new_advertisement(advertisement,text,wait_before_close)
		if advertisement and text and wait_before_close then
			if has_advertisement == false and can_da == false and frame_keybinds_mobile.Visible == false then
				unlockMouse()
				cool_gui_background = true
				can_da = true
				can_open_g_fr2 = false
				if enabled == true then
					can_open_g_fr = false
					game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)}):Play()
					wait(0.5)
					enabled = false
				end
				MobileButton.Visible = false
				frame5v2.Text = wait_before_close
				can_da = false
				D_text.Text = text
				T_text.Text = advertisement
				has_advertisement = true
				can_open_g_fr = false
				Advertisement_frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
				Advertisement_frame.Visible = true
				local size_all_scren = gui.AbsoluteSize.Y + gui.AbsoluteSize.X
				local del_size_all_scren = size_all_scren / 3
				local del_size_all_scren2 = size_all_scren / 5
				game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Advertisement_frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0, del_size_all_scren, 0, del_size_all_scren2)}):Play()
				for i = 1, wait_before_close do
					wait(1)
					frame5v2.Text = wait_before_close - i
					if i == wait_before_close then
						frame5v2.Text = "Ok"
						close_if_click_adv = true	
					end
				end	
				can_open_g_fr2 = true
			else

			end
		end
	end


	frame5v2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if close_if_click_adv == true then
			UI_SOUND_EZKA:Play()
			cool_gui_background = false
			close_if_click_adv = false
			lockMouse()
			MobileButton.Visible = true

		end
	end)

	local function open_keybinds()
		if Keybinds_frame.Visible == false then
			cool_gui_background = true
			can_open_g_fr = false
			MobileButton.Visible = false
			Keybinds_frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)}):Play()
			wait(0.5)
			Keybinds_frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			ccsk = true
			can_open_g_fr = true
			enabled = false
			Keybinds_frame.Visible = true
			game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Keybinds_frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = k_size}):Play()
		end
	end

	local already_listen = false
	local listen_keybind = nil

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if gameProcessed then return end
		if already_listen == true and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			listen_keybind.Text = "Keybind: "..input.KeyCode.Name

			already_listen = false
			if listen_keybind.Name == "1" then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/keybind.txt",input.KeyCode.Name)
			end
			if listen_keybind.Name == "2" then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/keybind.txt",input.KeyCode.Name)
			end
			if listen_keybind.Name == "3" then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/keybind.txt",input.KeyCode.Name)
			end
			if listen_keybind.Name == "4" then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/keybind.txt",input.KeyCode.Name)
			end
			listen_keybind = nil
			return
		end
	end)
	make_blur(Keybinds_frame)
	local function new_keybind(n)
		local keybinds_frame1 = Instance.new("Frame")
		keybinds_frame1.Parent = Keybinds_frame
		keybinds_frame1.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.13, 0)
		if n == 1 then
			keybinds_frame1.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.2, 0)
		elseif n == 2 then
			keybinds_frame1.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.35, 0)
		elseif n == 3 then
			keybinds_frame1.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		elseif n == 4 then
			keybinds_frame1.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.65, 0)
		end
		keybinds_frame1.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		keybinds_frame1.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		keybinds_frame1.BackgroundColor3 = color4
		Instance.new("UICorner", keybinds_frame1)
		local keybinds_frame1_text = Instance.new("TextButton")
		keybinds_frame1_text.Parent = keybinds_frame1
		keybinds_frame1_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0)
		keybinds_frame1_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		keybinds_frame1_text.Text = "Keybind "..n.." (Click to reset)"
		keybinds_frame1_text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		keybinds_frame1_text.TextScaled = true
		keybinds_frame1_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		keybinds_frame1_text.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		keybinds_frame1_text.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma

		local key = Instance.new("TextButton")
		key.Parent = keybinds_frame1
		key.Size = UDim2.new(0.27, 0, 1, 0)
		key.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		key.BackgroundColor3 = color3
		key.BorderSizePixel = 0
		key.Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 0)
		key.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		key.Text = "Keybind: Empty"
		key.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		key.TextScaled = true
		key.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		key.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		key.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
		key.Name = n
		Instance.new("UICorner", key)
		if mobile == true then
			key.Visible = false
		end

		local funct = Instance.new("TextBox")
		funct.Parent = keybinds_frame1
		funct.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0)
		funct.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		funct.BackgroundColor3 = color3
		funct.BorderSizePixel = 0
		funct.PlaceholderText = "Function (Click to select)"
		funct.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(124, 124, 124)
		funct.Position = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 0, 0)
		funct.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		funct.Text = ""
		funct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		funct.TextScaled = true
		funct.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		funct.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		funct.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma

		keybinds_frame1_text.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			key.Text = "Keybind: nothing"
			funct.Text = ""
			already_listen = false
			listen_keybind = nil
			if n == 1 then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/function.txt", "nothing")
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/keybind.txt", "nothing")
			end
			if n == 2 then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/function.txt", "nothing")
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/keybind.txt", "nothing")
			end
			if n == 3 then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/function.txt", "nothing")
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/keybind.txt", "nothing")
			end
			if n == 4 then
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/function.txt", "nothing")
				writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/keybind.txt", "nothing")
			end
		end)



		if n == 1 then
			key.Text = "Keybind: "..readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/keybind.txt")
		end
		if n == 2 then
			key.Text = "Keybind: "..readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/keybind.txt")
		end
		if n == 3 then
			key.Text = "Keybind: "..readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/keybind.txt")
		end
		if n == 4 then
			key.Text = "Keybind: "..readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/keybind.txt")
		end

		key.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if already_listen == false then
				UI_SOUND_EZKA:Play()
				already_listen = true
				key.Text = "Listen..."
				listen_keybind = key
				writefile("ezka/cfg/cfg4/cfg.txt","nothing")
			end	
		end)

		if n == 1 then
			if readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/function.txt") ~= "nothing" then
				funct.Text = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/function.txt")
			end
		end
		if n == 2 then
			if readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/function.txt") ~= "nothing" then
				funct.Text = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/function.txt")
			end
		end
		if n == 3 then
			if readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/function.txt") ~= "nothing" then
				funct.Text = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/function.txt")
			end
		end
		if n == 4 then
			if readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/function.txt") ~= "nothing" then
				funct.Text = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/function.txt")
			end
		end
		Instance.new("UICorner", funct)

		funct.Changed:Connect(function()
			funct.Changed:Connect(function()
				local text = funct.Text
				text = text:lower()
				text = string.gsub(text, "^%s+", "")
				text = text:gsub("[^a-z%s()123/,]", "")

				if funct.Text ~= text then
					funct.Text = text
				end
			end)

			if funct.Text ~= "" then
				local kjahbd = string.lower(funct.Text)
				if n == 1 then
					writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/function.txt", kjahbd)
				end
				if n == 2 then
					writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/function.txt", kjahbd)
				end
				if n == 3 then
					writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/function.txt", kjahbd)
				end
				if n == 4 then
					writefile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/function.txt", kjahbd)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	new_keybind(1)
	new_keybind(2)
	new_keybind(3)
	new_keybind(4)



	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input,gameProcessedEvent)
		if gameProcessedEvent then return end
		local keycode1 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/keybind.txt")
		local keycode2 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/keybind.txt")
		local keycode3 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/keybind.txt")
		local keycode4 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/keybind.txt")

		local function1 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind1/function.txt")
		local function2 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind2/function.txt")
		local function3 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind3/function.txt")
		local function4 = readfile("ezka/keybinds/keybind4/function.txt")
		local function1 = string.lower(function1)
		local function2 = string.lower(function2)
		local function3 = string.lower(function3)
		local function4 = string.lower(function4)

		if input.KeyCode.Name == keycode1 then
			if function1 == "Gui" or function1 == "gui" or function1 == "GUI" then
				Open_Gui_keybind_name = keycode1
			else
				local sadda = string.lower(function1)
				local a = all_functions_names[sadda]
				if a then  
					if values[a] ~= true then 
						values[a] = true
						new_notification(function1.." is enabled/used")
					else
						values[a] = false
						new_notification(function1.." is disabled")
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if input.KeyCode.Name == keycode2 then
			if function2 == "Gui" or function2 == "gui" or function2 == "GUI" then
				Open_Gui_keybind_name = keycode2
			else
				local sadda = string.lower(function2)
				local a = all_functions_names[sadda]
				if a then 
					if values[a] ~= true then 
						values[a] = true
						new_notification(function2.." is enabled/used")
					else
						values[a] = false
						new_notification(function2.." is disabled")
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if input.KeyCode.Name == keycode3 then
			if function3 == "Gui" or function3 == "gui" or function3 == "GUI" then
				Open_Gui_keybind_name = keycode3
			else
				local sadda = string.lower(function3)
				local a = all_functions_names[sadda]
				if a then 
					if values[a] ~= true then 
						values[a] = true
						new_notification(function3.." is enabled/used")
					else
						values[a] = false
						new_notification(function3.." is disabled")
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if input.KeyCode.Name == keycode4 then
			if function4 == "Gui" or function4 == "gui" or function4 == "GUI" then
				Open_Gui_keybind_name = keycode4
			else
				local sadda = string.lower(function4)
				local a = all_functions_names[sadda]
				if a then 
					if values[a] ~= true then 
						values[a] = true
						new_notification(function4.." is enabled/used")
					else
						values[a] = false
						new_notification(function4.." is disabled")
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if cd_rdahb == false then
			u_1oak_1(gui)
			u_1oak_1(Advertisement_frame)
			u_1oak_1(Keybinds_frame)
			u_1oak_1(notification_gui)
		end
	end)

	--new_tab("Gui Theme", true)


	function new_tab(name, vip, no, keybinds, iconi)
		if not name then return end
		
		if not iconi then
			iconi = "rbxassetid://93012434371030"
		end
		
		local tab
		tab = Instance.new("TextButton")
		tab.Parent = tabs
		tab.Text = ""
		tab.BackgroundColor3 = color4
		tab.BorderSizePixel = 0
		tab.TextColor3 = color2
		tab.TextScaled = true
		tab.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		tab.BackgroundTransparency = gui_transparency3
		tab.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		icon.Parent = tab
		icon.Size = UDim2.new(0.15, 0, 0.9, 0)
		icon.Name = "icon"
		icon.Image = iconi
		icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.98, 0, 0.5, 0)
		icon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
		icon.ImageColor3 = color1
		
		local uia = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
		uia.Parent = icon
		
		local tab_text 
		tab_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		tab_text.Parent = tab
		tab_text.Text = name
		tab_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		tab_text.BorderSizePixel = 0
		tab_text.TextColor3 = color2
		tab_text.TextScaled = true
		tab_text.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
		local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
		UICorner.Parent = tab
		UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.2, 0)
		tab_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		tab_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 1, 0)
		local tab_frame
		tab_frame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
		tab_frame.Parent = frame3
		tab_frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.9, 0)
		tab_frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.1, 0)
		tab_frame.Transparency = 1
		tab_frame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = color1
		tab_frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		tab_frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		tab_frame.Visible = false
		tab_frame.Name = name
		tab_frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 10, 0)

		local start_frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		start_frame.Parent = tab_frame
		start_frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.002, 0)
		start_frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		start_frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.001, 0)
		start_frame.Name = 1
		Instance.new("UICorner", tab_frame)
		Instance.new("UIGradient", tab_frame).Color = ColorSequence.new(color4, color3)
		local no_frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		no_frame.Parent = gui
		no_frame.Size = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.2, 0)
		no_frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		no_frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		no_frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		no_frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		no_frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		no_frame.Visible = false
		local UIAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
		UIAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = no_frame
		local no_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		no_text.Parent = no_frame
		no_text.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		no_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		no_text.Text = "Something went wrong, and this tab is broken."
		no_text.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
		no_text.TextScaled = true
		no_text.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
		no_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		no_text.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		if no ~= true then
			no_frame:Destroy()
		end

		if vip == true and vip_version == false then
			tab_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0)
			icon:Destroy()
			local vip_text 
			vip_text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			vip_text.Parent = tab
			vip_text.Text = "VIP"
			vip_text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			vip_text.BorderSizePixel = 0
			vip_text.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			vip_text.TextScaled = true
			vip_text.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
			vip_text.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0)
			vip_text.Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 0)
			vip_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
			Instance.new("UIGradient", vip_text).Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 0.945098, 0.662745), Color3.new(0.678431, 0.494118, 0.0196078))
		end
		tab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			UI_SOUND_EZKA:Play()
			if no == true then
				if no_frame.Visible == false then
					no_frame.Visible = true
					wait(3)
					no_frame.Visible = false
				end
			else
				if vip == true and vip_version == false then
					new_advertisement("You don't have a VIP key.","You tried to open a tab that requires a VIP version of the script, but you do not have a VIP version of the script activated.",3)
				else

					if keybinds == true then
						open_keybinds()
					else
						if tab_frame.Visible == true then

						else
							t.Text = tab_text.Text
							for _, v in pairs(frame3:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
									v.Visible = false
								end
							end
							tab_frame.Visible = true
							select_tab.Value = tab
							for _, v in pairs(tabs:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("TextButton") then
									local d = v:FindFirstChild("icon")
									if d then
										game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(d, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {ImageColor3 = color1}):Play()
									end
									
									game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(v.TextLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {TextColor3 = color2}):Play()
									game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {BackgroundColor3 = color4}):Play()
								end
							end
							local d = select_tab.Value:FindFirstChild("icon")
							if d then
								game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(d, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)}):Play()
							end
							game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(select_tab.Value.TextLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {TextColor3 = color7}):Play()
							game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(select_tab.Value, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {BackgroundColor3 = color2}):Play()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end




	function make_place(arguments_table)
		local place_type = arguments_table.place_type
		local parent_tab = arguments_table.parent_tab
		local place_size = arguments_table.place_size
		local place_have_title = arguments_table.place_have_title
		local place_have_text = arguments_table.place_have_text
		local place_text = arguments_table.place_text
		local title_text = arguments_table.title_text
		local place_have_image = arguments_table.place_have_image
		local local_value_name = arguments_table.local_value_name
		local max_number = arguments_table.max_number
		local first_number = arguments_table.first_number
		local beta = arguments_table.beta
		local auto_farm = arguments_table.auto_farm
		local auto_badges = arguments_table.auto_badges
		local vip_1 = arguments_table.vip_1
		if not first_number then
			first_number = 1
		end



		local parent_tab_local = frame3:FindFirstChild(parent_tab)
		if place_type == "separator" then
			local place = Instance.new("Frame")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			local fp = Instance.new("Frame")
			fp.Name = "fp"
			fp.Parent = place
			fp.BorderSizePixel = 0
			fp.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			fp.Size = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0.5, 0)
			fp.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
			fp.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			fp.BackgroundColor3 = color3
			local UIGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
			UIGradient.Parent = fp
			UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.0, 1),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0.3),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.3),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1.0, 1)
			})

			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.0001
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.0035, 0)
		end
		if place_type == "marked" then
			local place = Instance.new("Frame")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			Instance.new("UICorner", place)
			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.0005
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.01, 0)
			local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			TextLabel.Parent = place
			TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0, 0)
			TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 1, 0)
			TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = color1
			TextLabel.TextScaled = true
			TextLabel.Text = arguments_table.place_text
			TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			image.Parent = place
			image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			image.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
			image.Size = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 1, 0)
			image.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			image.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=88919184224923"
			image.ImageColor3 = color1
			local GuiAspectRatio = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
			GuiAspectRatio.Parent = image
		end
		if place_type == "title" then
			local place = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			place.Text = title_text
			place.TextScaled = true
			place.TextColor3 = color1
			place.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
			Instance.new("UICorner", place)
			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.0001
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.02, 0)
		end
		if place_type == "billboard" then
			local place = Instance.new("Frame")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundColor3 = color3
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
			Instance.new("UICorner", place)
			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.001
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, place_size, 0)
			if place_have_title == true then
				local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				title.Parent = place
				title.Text = title_text
				title.TextColor3 = color1
				title.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.2, 0)
				title.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
				title.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.01, 0)
				title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				title.TextScaled = true
			end
			if place_have_text == true then
				local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				text.Parent = place
				text.Text = place_text
				text.TextColor3 = color2
				text.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.8, 0)
				text.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
				text.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.2, 0)
				text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				text.TextScaled = true
			end	
		end
		if place_type == "slider" then
			local place = Instance.new("TextButton")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundColor3 = color8
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
			place.Text = ""
			local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			title.Parent = place
			title.Text = title_text
			title.TextColor3 = color1
			title.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 1, 0)
			title.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
			title.Position = UDim2.new(1.1, 0, 0, 0)
			title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			title.TextScaled = true
			title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
			if beta == true then
				title.Size = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 1, 0)
				local betatitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				betatitle.Parent = place
				betatitle.Text = "BETA"
				betatitle.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 0.666667, 0)
				betatitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0)
				betatitle.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
				betatitle.Position = UDim2.new(1.9, 0, 0, 0)
				betatitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				betatitle.TextScaled = true
				betatitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			end
			values[local_value_name] = false
			local sadda = string.lower(title_text)
			all_functions_names[sadda] = local_value_name

			local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
			UICorner.Parent = place
			UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.2, 0)

			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.001
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.010, 0)
			local slider_button = Instance.new("Frame")
			slider_button.Parent = place
			slider_button.BackgroundColor3 = color1
			slider_button.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.7, 0)
			slider_button.Position= UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.15, 0)
			if vip_1 == true and vip_version == false then
				local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
				image.Parent = slider_button
				image.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11322089611"
				image.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.9, 0)
				image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				image.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
				image.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				image.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 0.784314, 0)
				local UIAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
				UIAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = image
			end
			Instance.new("UICorner", slider_button)
			local Value
			place.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				UI_SOUND_EZKA:Play()
				if values[local_value_name] == true then
					if auto_farm ~= true then
						values[local_value_name] = false
						place.BackgroundColor3 = color8
						game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(slider_button, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.15, 0)}):Play()					
					end
				else
					if vip_1 == true and vip_version == false then
						new_advertisement("You don't have a VIP key.","You tried to use a function that requires a VIP version of the script, but you do not have a VIP version of the script activated.",2)
					else
						if auto_badges == true then
							if game.PlaceId == 14437001043 then
								if support_fire_click_detector == false then
									badge_Frame.Visible = true
									game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(badge_Frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)}):Play()
									wait(0.5)
									can_t_bf = false
								else
									new_advertisement("Bad Executor","Your executor does not support this feature. Try to find a really good executor. I know that this feature works on Delta executor.",4)
								end
							else
								new_advertisement("Wrong Place!","To auto farm, you need to be in the lobby!",2)
							end
						else
							if auto_farm ~= true then
								values[local_value_name] = true
								place.BackgroundColor3 = color2
								game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(slider_button, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0.15, 0)}):Play()
							else
								if readfile("ezka/auto_farm.txt") == "false" then
									if game.PlaceId == 14437001043 then
										if support_fire_click_detector == true then
											local Lobbies = workspace.Lobbies
											local find_a_lobby = false
											for i,v in pairs(Lobbies:GetChildren()) do
												if find_a_lobby == false then
													local Owner = v:FindFirstChild("Owner")
													if Owner.Value == "" then
														find_a_lobby = true
														writefile("ezka/auto_farm.txt","true")
														place.BackgroundColor3 = color2
														game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(slider_button, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0.15, 0)}):Play()
														game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v.PlayersNum.CFrame
														wait(0.35)
														writefile("ezka/auto_farm.txt","true")
														local LobbyUI = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.LobbyUI
														LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
															Total = 1
														})
														if values["auto_farm_night2"] == true then
															LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
																Night = "Night2"
															})
														else
															LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
																Night = "Night1"
															})

														end
														if values["auto_farm_night2"] == false then
															LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
																Modifier = {"Thunderstorm", true}
															})
														end
														LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
															Modifier = {"Bloodmoon", true}
														})
														LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
															Modifier = {"Graves", true}
														})
														LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
															Modifier = {"Blizzard", true}
														})
														LobbyUI:FireServer(false,{
															Start = true
														})
														writefile("ezka/auto_farm.txt","true")
													end
												end
											end
										else
											new_advertisement("Bad Executor","Your executor does not support this feature. Try to find a really good executor. I know that this feature works on Delta executor.",4)
										end
									else
										writefile("ezka/auto_farm.txt","false")
										new_advertisement("Wrong Place!","To auto farm, you need to be in the lobby!",2)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			local cdad = false
			game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if auto_farm ~= true then
					if cdad == false then
						cdad = true
						if values[local_value_name] == true then
							place.BackgroundColor3 = color2
							game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(slider_button, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0.15, 0)}):Play()
						else
							place.BackgroundColor3 = color8
							game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(slider_button, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.15, 0)}):Play()
						end
						wait(0.5)
						cdad = false
					end
				end
			end)


		end
		if place_type == "sub_slider" then
			local place = Instance.new("TextButton")
			local maxNum = 0
			for _, v in ipairs(parent_tab_local:GetChildren()) do 
				local num = tonumber(v.Name:match("%d+"))
				if num and num > maxNum then 
					maxNum = num
					bestObject = v
				end
			end
			local can_c = true
			values[local_value_name] = false
			local sadda = string.lower(title_text)
			all_functions_names[sadda] = local_value_name
			place.Name = maxNum + 1
			place.Parent = parent_tab_local
			place.BorderSizePixel = 0
			place.BackgroundColor3 = color8
			place.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
			place.Text = ""
			Instance.new("UICorner", place)
			half_best_obj_size = bestObject.Size.Y.Scale
			new_position_place = bestObject.Position.Y.Scale + 0.001
			full_position_new_place = new_position_place + half_best_obj_size
			place.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, full_position_new_place , 0)
			place.Size = UDim2.new(0.08, 0, 0.008, 0)
			local UIAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
			UIAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = place

			local function updateSliderAndText()
				local current_value = values[local_value_name] or 0
				local max_drag_position_scale = 1 - drag.Size.X.Scale 
				local drag_pos_scale = (current_value / max_number) * max_drag_position_scale
				drag.Position = UDim2.new(drag_pos_scale, 0, 0, 0)
				title.Text = tostring(math.round(current_value))
				if gui_s1_sl == false then
					UIDrag.MaxDragTranslation = UDim2.new(max_drag_position_scale - drag.Position.X.Scale, 0, 0, 0)
					UIDrag.MinDragTranslation = UDim2.new(0 - drag.Position.X.Scale, 0, 0, 0)
				else
					UIDrag.MaxDragTranslation = UDim2.new(500, 0, 0, 0)
					UIDrag.MinDragTranslation = UDim2.new(500, 0, 0, 0)
				end
			end
			updateSliderAndText()
			UIDrag.DragEnd:Connect(function()
				local parentSize = place.AbsoluteSize.X
				local current_drag_scale = drag.Position.X.Scale
				local drag_offset_ratio = drag.Position.X.Offset / parentSize
				local final_drag_scale = current_drag_scale + drag_offset_ratio
				local max_drag_position_scale = 1 - drag.Size.X.Scale
				local corrected_scaleX = math.clamp(final_drag_scale, 0, max_drag_position_scale)
				drag.Position = UDim2.new(corrected_scaleX, 0, 0, 0)
				if gui_s1_sl == false then
					UIDrag.MaxDragTranslation = UDim2.new(max_drag_position_scale - corrected_scaleX, 0, 0, 0)
					UIDrag.MinDragTranslation = UDim2.new(0 - corrected_scaleX, 0, 0, 0)
				else
					UIDrag.MaxDragTranslation = UDim2.new(500, 0, 0, 0)
					UIDrag.MinDragTranslation = UDim2.new(500, 0, 0, 0)
				end
				local conv_val = (corrected_scaleX / max_drag_position_scale) * max_number
				values[local_value_name] = math.round(conv_val)
				title.Text = tostring(math.round(conv_val))
			end)

						local itog = ""
						local function tableToString(inputTable)
							local parts = {}
							for key, value in pairs(inputTable) do
								local formattedKey = tostring(key)
								local formattedValue
								if type(value) == "string" then
									formattedValue = '"' .. value .. '"'
								elseif type(value) == "number" then
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								elseif type(value) == "boolean" then
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								elseif type(value) == "table" then
									formattedValue = "[Table]"
								else
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								end
								table.insert(parts, formattedKey .. ":" .. formattedValue)
							end
							return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
						end
						itog = tableToString(values)
						writefile("ezka/cfg/cfg1/cfg.txt",itog)
					end

								table.insert(parts, formattedKey .. ":" .. formattedValue)
							end
							return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
						end
						itog = tableToString(values)
						writefile("ezka/cfg/cfg3/cfg.txt",itog)
					end
					if local_value_name == "cfg4" then
						local itog = ""
						local function tableToString(inputTable)
							local parts = {}
							for key, value in pairs(inputTable) do
								local formattedKey = tostring(key)
								local formattedValue
								if type(value) == "string" then
									formattedValue = '"' .. value .. '"'
								elseif type(value) == "number" then
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								elseif type(value) == "boolean" then
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								elseif type(value) == "table" then
									formattedValue = "[Table]"
								else
									formattedValue = tostring(value)
								end
								table.insert(parts, formattedKey .. ":" .. formattedValue)
							end
							return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
						end
						itog = tableToString(values)
						writefile("ezka/cfg/cfg4/cfg.txt",itog)
					end
					new_notification("You have successfully saved the configuration slot!")
				end
			end)

			load_button.Parent = place
			load_button.BackgroundColor3 = color1
			load_button.Size = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.9, 0)

					local itog = readfile("ezka/cfg/cfg1/cfg.txt")
					local cleaned = itog:gsub("[{}]", "")
					local pairs = {}
					for pair in cleaned:gmatch("([^,]+)") do
						table.insert(pairs, pair)
					end
					for _, pair in ipairs(pairs) do
						local key, value = pair:match("([^:]+):([^:]+)")
						if key and value then
							key = key:gsub('"', ''):gsub("'", "")
							value = value:gsub('"', ''):gsub("'", "")
							if value == "true" then
								values[key] = true
							elseif value == "false" then
								values[key] = false
							elseif tonumber(value) then
								values[key] = tonumber(value)
							else
								values[key] = value
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if local_value_name == "cfg2" then

							end
						end
					end

									local hasI = CandyBowl:FindFirstChild("1"):WaitForChild("Decal").Transparency == 0 or CandyBowl:FindFirstChild("2"):WaitForChild("Decal").Transparency == 0 or CandyBowl:FindFirstChild("3"):WaitForChild("Decal").Transparency == 0

										local CandyBowlClick = CandyBowl:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")
										if CandyBowlClick then
											fireclickdetector(CandyBowlClick)
										end
									end
								end
							end
							local FrontDoor = workspace:FindFirstChild("FrontDoor")
							if FrontDoor then
								local SoundPart = FrontDoor:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
								if SoundPart then
									local KSound = SoundPart:FindFirstChild("DoorKnock")
									if KSound then
										if KSound.Playing == true then
											local Hitbox = FrontDoor:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
											if Hitbox then
												local HitboxClick = Hitbox:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")
												if HitboxClick then
													stop_working_mans_auto = true
													fireclickdetector(HitboxClick)
													wait(1)
													local is_kid = nil
													local Growling = SoundPart:FindFirstChild("Growling")
													if Growling then
														if Growling.Playing == true then
															is_kid = false
														else
															is_kid = true
														end
													end
													can_door = false
													local DialogueUI = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("DialogueUI")
													if is_kid == true then
														DialogueUI.IgnoreButton.Visible = false
														DialogueUI.OpenButton.Visible = false
														game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Choice:FireServer(true)
													else
														DialogueUI.IgnoreButton.Visible = false
														DialogueUI.OpenButton.Visible = false
														game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Choice:FireServer(false)
													end
													wait(7)
													stop_working_mans_auto = false
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end

						end
					end
				else
					values["auto_mansion_incident"] = false
					new_advertisement("Bad Executor","Your executor does not support this feature. Try to find a really good executor. I know that this feature works on Delta executor.",4)

				local Players = game:GetService("Players")
				local player = Players.LocalPlayer
				local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

				local maxRayDistance = 30


				if not player or not player.Character or not camera then
					return
				end

				local rayOrigin = camera.CFrame.Position
				local rayDirection = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
				raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
				raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {player.Character}
				raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true

				local result = workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDirection * maxRayDistance, raycastParams)

				if result then
					local obj = result.Instance
					while obj and obj.Name == "BasePart" do
						obj = obj.Parent
					end
					if obj then
						if values["auto_shoot_shotgun_mutant"] == true then
							if obj.Parent.Name == "Mutant" then
								local Item = player.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
								if Item then
									if Item.Name ~= "Shotgun" then
										Item.Parent = player.Backpack
									end
								end
								local Shotgun = player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Shotgun")
								if Shotgun then
									Shotgun.Parent = player.Character
								end
							end
						end 
						if values["auto_shoot_shotgun_spider"] == true then
							if obj.Parent.Name == "WorkerHead" then

												end
											end

											if Shotgun then
												Shotgun.Parent = player.Character
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

	function isDescendantOf(child, ancestor)
		if not child or not ancestor then return false end

		local current = child

		while current do

			if current == ancestor then
				return true
			end

			if not current.Parent then
				break
			end
			current = current.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	function new_esp(value,color,name,value_gui)
		if not value or not color or not name then
			return
		end
		local FIRST_VALUE = value
		if value:IsA("Model") then
			if value.PrimaryPart then
				value = value.PrimaryPart
			else
				for i,v in pairs(value:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("BasePart") then
						value = v
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local framedagg = Instance.new("Frame")
		framedagg.Parent = Screen1489
		framedagg.Name = name
		framedagg.BackgroundColor3 = color
		framedagg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		framedagg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		framedagg.Size = UDim2.new(0.01, 0, 0.01, 0)
		framedagg.Visible = false
		framedagg.BorderSizePixel = 0

		dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

		dot.BackgroundTransparency = 1

			local yv = values["y_trasers"] / 100
			dot2.Position = UDim2.new(xv, 0, yv, 0)
		end)
		game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if value then
				if FIRST_VALUE then
					if values[value_gui] == true then
						local targetPart = value	
						if targetPart then
							local isChildOfWorkspace = isDescendantOf(targetPart, workspace)
							if isChildOfWorkspace then
								if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight") then
									FIRST_VALUE.ScriptHighlight:Destroy()
								end
								if not FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight") then
									local Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
									Highlight.Name = "ScriptHighlight"
									Highlight.FillColor = color
									Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.6
									Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 1
									Highlight.Parent = FIRST_VALUE
								end
								if not FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag") then
									local NameTag = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
									NameTag.Name = "ScriptNameTag"
									NameTag.Size = UDim2.new(5,0,2,0)
									NameTag.AlwaysOnTop = true
									NameTag.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
									if value_gui == "esp_players" then
										NameTag.Enabled = false
									end
									NameTag.Parent = FIRST_VALUE
									local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
									TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1.5, 0, 1.5, 0)
									TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
									TextLabel.Text = name
									TextLabel.TextColor3 = color
									TextLabel.TextScaled = true
									TextLabel.Parent = NameTag

								end
								framedagg.Transparency = values["esp_trasers_transparency"] / 10
								if targetPart:IsA("Model") and not targetPart.PrimaryPart then
									local foundPart = targetPart:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
									if foundPart then
										targetPart.PrimaryPart = foundPart
									end
								end
								if targetPart:IsA("Model") and targetPart.PrimaryPart then

									targetPart = targetPart.PrimaryPart
								else
								end
								if FIRST_VALUE then
									if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight") then
										FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight").Enabled = true
									end
									if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag") then
										local tage = FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag")
										if value_gui ~= "esp_players" and values["disable_esp_name_tags"] == false then
											FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag").Enabled = true
										else
											FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag").Enabled = false
										end
										local playerPosition = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										if playerPosition then
											local distance = (playerPosition.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude
											tage.TextLabel.Text = string.format("%s [%.2f]", name, distance)
										end
									end
								end
								if values["disable_esp_trasers"] == false then
									if targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then
										local worldPos = targetPart.Position
										local vector, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
										if onScreen then
											framedagg.Visible = true
											local frameA = dot
											local frameB = dot2
											local centerA = frameA.AbsolutePosition + (frameA.AbsoluteSize / 2)
											local centerB = frameB.AbsolutePosition + (frameB.AbsoluteSize / 2)
											local parentPos = framedagg.Parent.AbsolutePosition
											local posA = centerA - parentPos
											local posB = centerB - parentPos
											local delta = centerB - centerA
											local distance = delta.Magnitude
											local angle = math.atan2(delta.Y, delta.X)
											framedagg.Size = UDim2.new(0, distance, 0.001, 0)
											framedagg.Position = UDim2.new(0, (posA.X + posB.X) / 2, 0, (posA.Y + posB.Y) / 2)
											framedagg.Rotation = math.deg(angle)
											framedagg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
											framedagg.BorderSizePixel = 0
											dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, vector.X, 0, vector.Y)
										else
											framedagg.Visible = false
										end
									end
								else
									framedagg.Visible = false
								end
								local xsize = framedagg.Size.X.Offset
								framedagg.Size = UDim2.new(0, xsize, 0.01 * values["esp_trasers_thickness"] / 10, 0)
							else
								framedagg.Visible = false
							end
							if game.PlaceId == 100255403764514 then
								if FIRST_VALUE.Name == "JerryCan" then
									if targetPart.Transparency == 1 then
										framedagg.Visible = false
										if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag") then
											FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag").Enabled = false
										end
									end
								end
							end
						else
							framedagg.Visible = false
						end
					else
						framedagg.Visible = false	
						if FIRST_VALUE then
							if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight") then
								FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptHighlight").Enabled = false
							end
							if FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag") then
								FIRST_VALUE:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameTag").Enabled = false
							end
						end

					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

			end
		end)
	end

					new_notification("Larry Spawned!", "🚨", Color3.new(1, 0.760784, 0.760784))
				end
				wasInRadius = false
			end

			lastKnownMutantPosition = currentMutantPosition
		end)
	end

	make_place({
		place_type = "button",
		title_text = "Teleport to best ammo spot (night3)",
		parent_tab = "Teleports",
		local_value_name = "tp_a3",
		vip_1 = true
	}
	)
	if game.PlaceId == 140260816701736 then
		game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if values["tp_a3"] == true then
				values["tp_a3"] = false
				local Players = game:GetService("Players")
				local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
				local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
				local character = LocalPlayer.Character
				local humanoidRootPart = character and character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local primaryMutantPart = nil
				local targetDetector = nil
				local isTeleported = false
				local originalCFrame = nil
				local teleportDuration = 1.5
				local function getAllDetectors()
					local detectors = {}
					local ammoPiles = Workspace:FindFirstChild("AmmoPiles")
					if ammoPiles then
						for _, ammoPile in ipairs(ammoPiles:GetChildren()) do
							local detector = ammoPile:FindFirstChild("Detector")
							if detector then
								table.insert(detectors, detector)
							end
						end
					end
					return detectors
				end
				local function findMutant()
					local mutant = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Mutant")
					if mutant and mutant:IsA("Model") then
						local primary = mutant:FindFirstChild("PrimaryPart")
						if primary then
							return primary
						elseif mutant:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							return mutant:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						else
							local parts = mutant:GetDescendants()
							local totalPosition = Vector3.zero
							local count = 0
							for _, instance in ipairs(parts) do
								if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
									totalPosition += instance.Position
									count += 1
								end
							end
							if count > 0 then
								return CFrame.new(totalPosition / count)
							end
						end
					end
					return nil
				end
				local function returnPlayer()
					if isTeleported and originalCFrame and humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
						humanoidRootPart.CFrame = originalCFrame
						isTeleported = false
						originalCFrame = nil
					elseif isTeleported and (not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart.Parent) then
						isTeleported = false
						originalCFrame = nil
					end
				end
				local function teleportPlayer(targetCFrame)
					if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
						originalCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
						humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
						isTeleported = true
						task.wait(teleportDuration)
						returnPlayer()
					end
				end

				if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
					local detectors = getAllDetectors()
					if #detectors > 0 and not isTeleported then
						primaryMutantPart = findMutant()
						if primaryMutantPart then
							local farthestDistance = 0
							local farthestDetector = nil
							for _, detector in ipairs(detectors) do
								local detectorPosition = detector.CFrame.Position
								local mutantPosition = primaryMutantPart.CFrame.Position
								local distance = (detectorPosition - mutantPosition).Magnitude
								if distance > farthestDistance then
									farthestDistance = distance
									farthestDetector = detector
								end
							end
							if farthestDetector then
								teleportPlayer(farthestDetector.CFrame)
							end
						else
							local closestDistance = math.huge
							local closestDetector = nil
							local playerPosition = humanoidRootPart.Position
							for _, detector in ipairs(detectors) do
								local detectorPosition = detector.CFrame.Position
								local distance = (detectorPosition - playerPosition).Magnitude
								if distance < closestDistance then
									closestDistance = distance
									closestDetector = detector
								end
							end
							if closestDetector then
								teleportPlayer(closestDetector.CFrame)
							end
						end
					end

				end
			end
		end)
	end

						table.insert(pairs, pair)
					end
					for _, pair in ipairs(pairs) do
						local key, value = pair:match("([^:]+):([^:]+)")
						if key and value then
							key = key:gsub('"', ''):gsub("'", "")
							value = value:gsub('"', ''):gsub("'", "")

								values[key] = true
							elseif value == "false" then
								values[key] = false

	local Smart_Teleport_Hightlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
	Smart_Teleport_Hightlight.Parent = nil
	Smart_Teleport_Hightlight.FillTransparency = 0.8
	Smart_Teleport_Hightlight.FillColor = Color3.new(0.564706, 0.796078, 1)
	Smart_Teleport_Hightlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.3

	local teleporting = false
	local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

	local function teleportPlayerN2(x, y, z)
		if not (x and y and z) then return end
		local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		local character = player.Character
		if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
		local humanoidRootPart = character.HumanoidRootPart
		local doorsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Doors")
		local ignoreList = {}
		if doorsFolder then
			for _, v in pairs(doorsFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "Door" then
					table.insert(ignoreList, v)
				end
			end
		end
		local targetPosition = Vector3.new(x, y, z)
		local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
			AgentRadius = 1.5,
			AgentHeight = 2,
			AgentCanJump = true,
			Costs = {
				["Door"] = 1
			}
		})
		local success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
			path:ComputeAsync(humanoidRootPart.Position, targetPosition)
		end)
		if success and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
			local waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
			if teleporting then return end
			teleporting = true
			for _, waypoint in pairs(waypoints) do
				local currentWaypointPosition = waypoint.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
				local targetCFrameForWaypoint = CFrame.new(currentWaypointPosition) * CFrame.Angles(humanoidRootPart.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesXYZ())
				local tween = TweenService:Create(humanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(0.06), {CFrame = targetCFrameForWaypoint})
				tween:Play()
				tween.Completed:Wait()
				if waypoint.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
				end
			end
			teleporting = false
		end
	end



	local function areAllPanelsSafe()
		local pressurePanels = workspace:FindFirstChild("PressurePanels")
		if not pressurePanels then return false end
		local panels = {}
		for _, child in ipairs(pressurePanels:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "Panel" then
				table.insert(panels, child)
			end
		end
		for _, panel in ipairs(panels) do
			local failing = panel:FindFirstChild("Failing")
			if not failing or failing.Value then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end


	local function teleportToNearestButton(player)
		local mutant = game.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Mutant")
		local floodlightsFolder = game.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Floodlights")
		if not mutant then return end
		if not floodlightsFolder then return end
		local primaryPart = mutant.PrimaryPart
		if not primaryPart then return end
		local nearestButton = nil
		local minDistance = math.huge
		for _, lightModel in pairs(floodlightsFolder:GetChildren()) do
			local switch = lightModel:FindFirstChild("Switch")
			if switch then
				local button = switch:FindFirstChild("Button")
				if button and button:IsA("BasePart") then
					local distance = (button.Position - primaryPart.Position).Magnitude
					if distance < minDistance then
						minDistance = distance
						nearestButton = button
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if nearestButton then
			local buttonPos = nearestButton.Position
			local targetPos = Vector3.new(buttonPos.X - 3, buttonPos.Y, buttonPos.Z)
			x = targetPos.X
			y = targetPos.Y
			z = targetPos.Z
			teleportPlayerN2(x, y, z)
		end
	end
	local function areAllProgressZero()
		local Floodlights = workspace:FindFirstChild("Floodlights")
		if not Floodlights then return false end

		for _, v in pairs(Floodlights:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "Light" then
				local Wire = v:FindFirstChild("Wire")
				local Hole = Wire and Wire:FindFirstChild("Hole")
				local Rat = Hole and Hole:FindFirstChild("Rat")
				local Progress = Rat and Rat:FindFirstChild("Progress")
				if Progress and Progress.Value ~= 0 then
					return false
				end
			end
		end
		return true
	end

		{pos = Vector3.new(-234.87498474121094, 85.70652770996094, -1.625), x = -232, y = 82.4, z = -0.48, name = "первому"},
