	local function elsev2()
		if areAllProgressZero() then
			if areAllPanelsSafe() then
				x = -282.8
				y = 82.4
				z = 17
				teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
			else
				local ItemCrate = workspace:FindFirstChild("ItemCrate")
				if ItemCrate then
					x = -148.6
					y = 82.4
					z = 65.2
					teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
				else
					local PressurePanels = workspace:FindFirstChild("PressurePanels")
					if PressurePanels then
						local targetInfo = nil
						for _, info in ipairs(panelPriority) do
							local foundPanel = nil
							for _, v in pairs(PressurePanels:GetChildren()) do
								if v.Name == "Panel" then
									local light = v:FindFirstChild("Light")
									local failing = v:FindFirstChild("Failing")
									if light and failing and failing.Value == true and light.Position == info.pos then
										foundPanel = info
										break
									end
								end
							end
							if foundPanel then
								targetInfo = foundPanel
								break
							end
						end
						if targetInfo then
							teleportPlayerN2(targetInfo.x, targetInfo.y, targetInfo.z)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		else
			local Floodlights = workspace:FindFirstChild("Floodlights")
			if Floodlights then
				for i,v in pairs(Floodlights:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Name == "Light" then
						local Wire = v:FindFirstChild("Wire")
						if Wire then
							local Hole = Wire:FindFirstChild("Hole")
							local Rat = Hole:FindFirstChild("Rat")
							if Rat then
								local Progress = Rat:FindFirstChild("Progress")
								if Progress then
									if Progress.Value >= 1 then
										local Outer = Wire:FindFirstChild("Outer")
										if Outer then
											if Outer.Position == Vector3.new(-292.14404296875, 104.99996948242188, -14.270751953125) or Outer.Position == Vector3.new(-321.64404296875, 104.99996948242188, -14.270751953125) then
												x = -307.5
												y = 82.4
												z = -18
												teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
											elseif Outer.Position == Vector3.new(-309.76904296875, 104.99996948242188, -90.395751953125) or Outer.Position == Vector3.new(-280.76904296875, 104.99996948242188, -90.395751953125) then
												x = -296
												y = 82.4
												z = -91.7
												teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

									if PressurePanels then
										local targetInfo = nil
										for _, info in ipairs(panelPriority) do
											local foundPanel = nil
											for _, v in pairs(PressurePanels:GetChildren()) do
												if v.Name == "Panel" then
													local light = v:FindFirstChild("Light")
													local failing = v:FindFirstChild("Failing")
													if light and failing and failing.Value == true and light.Position == info.pos then
														foundPanel = info
														break
													end
												end
											end
											if foundPanel then
												targetInfo = foundPanel
												break
											end
										end
										if targetInfo then
											teleportPlayerN2(targetInfo.x, targetInfo.y, targetInfo.z)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						elseif game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.DialogueUI.ObjectiveFrame.ObjectiveText.Text == "Survive the night." then
							local GameState = game.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GameState")
							if GameState then
								local Fuel = GameState:FindFirstChild("Fuel")
								if Fuel then
									if Fuel.Value <= 1 then
										local PowerCell = workspace:FindFirstChild("PowerCell")
										if PowerCell then
											local PowerCell = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("PowerCell")
											if not PowerCell then
												x = -320.6
												y = 82.4
												z = 97.7
												teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
											else
												x = -272
												y = 82.4
												z = 112.5
												teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
											end
										end
									else
										local Mutant = workspace:FindFirstChild("Mutant")
										if Mutant then
											teleportToNearestButton(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
										else
											local Stalker = workspace:FindFirstChild("Stalker")
											if Stalker then
												local UVLamp = nil
												local UVLamp1 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("UVLamp")
												local UVLamp2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("UVLamp")
												local sItem = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
												if UVLamp1 then
													UVLamp = UVLamp1
												end
												if UVLamp2 then
													UVLamp = UVLamp2
												end
												if UVLamp then
													x = Stalker.PrimaryPart.Position.X
													y = 82.4
													z = Stalker.PrimaryPart.Position.Z + 6
													teleportPlayerN2(x,y,z)
													if sItem then
														sItem.Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
														UVLamp.Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
													end
												else
													elsev2()
												end	
											else
												elsev2()
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
					if game.PlaceId == 100255403764514 then
						values["noclip"] = true
						if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.DialogueUI.ObjectiveFrame.ObjectiveText.Text == "Send a distress signal" then
							game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(16.4581375, 16.9999943, 71.8360291, -0.999961913, -3.0869753e-08, -0.00872720405, -3.1072144e-08, 1, 2.30550725e-08, 0.00872720405, 2.33253665e-08, -0.999961913)
						else
							local rat = workspace:FindFirstChild("BunkerRat", true)
							if rat then
								if rat.PrimaryPart and (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - rat.PrimaryPart.Position).Magnitude < 20  then
									game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(67.7495346, 18.2603893, 77.5174866, 0.998784363, -5.04894837e-09, -0.0492925607, 3.04780912e-09, 1, -4.06723473e-08, 0.0492925607, 4.04726705e-08, 0.998784363)
								else
									f2314()
								end
							else
								f2314()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

					l3 = v

					l4 = v

					l6 = v

			if l2 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "2. " .. tostring(l2) end

			if l5 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "5. " .. tostring(l5) end
			if l6 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "6. " .. tostring(l6) end
			if l7 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "7. " .. tostring(l7) end
			new_advertisement("Changelist ".."v"..versionsc, changelistString, 3)

		end

				l3 = v

				l6 = v

			end
		end

			if l2 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "2. " .. tostring(l2) end

			if l7 ~= nil then changelistString = changelistString .. (changelistString ~= "" and separator or "") .. "7. " .. tostring(l7) end

local function animateText(targetLabel, text, duration, delay)
	targetLabel.Text = ""
	local currentText = ""
	for i = 1, #text do
		currentText = currentText .. string.sub(text, i, i)
		targetLabel.Text = currentText
		wait(delay)
	end
end

local maxLength = 19
local function filterInput(input)
	local filteredText = string.gsub(input, "[^a-zA-Z_]", "")
	return filteredText
end
imput_box_t.Changed:Connect(function()
	local originalText = imput_box_t.Text
	local filteredText = filterInput(originalText)
	if #filteredText > maxLength then
		filteredText = string.sub(filteredText, 1, maxLength)
	end
	if imput_box_t.Text ~= filteredText then
		imput_box_t.Text = filteredText
	end
end)

	particlesFolder.Parent = gui

local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local function discord_boom()
	local count = 60
	local spread = 20
	local gravity = 20
	local lifetime = 5
	local centerX = discord_image.AbsolutePosition.X + (discord_image.AbsoluteSize.X / 2)
	local centerY = discord_image.AbsolutePosition.Y + (discord_image.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2)
	for _ = 1, count do
		local part = Instance.new("Frame")
		part.Size = UDim2.new(0.02, 0, 0.02, 0)
		local variant_cveta = math.random(1,3)
		if variant_cveta == 1 then
			part.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 176, 255)
		elseif variant_cveta == 2 then
			part.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 13, 255)
		elseif variant_cveta == 3 then
			part.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 14, 127)
		end
		part.BorderSizePixel = 0
		part.Parent = particlesFolder
		local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
		UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		UICorner.Parent = part
		local UIAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
		UIAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = part
		local angle = Random.new():NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2)
		local dist = Random.new():NextNumber(60, spread)
		local startOffsetX = math.cos(angle) * dist
		local startOffsetY = math.sin(angle) * dist
		part.Position = UDim2.new(0, centerX + startOffsetX - 4, 0, centerY + startOffsetY - 4)
		local speedX = startOffsetX * 0.3
		local speedY = startOffsetY * 0.3
		local createdTime = tick()
		local connection
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
			speedY = speedY + gravity * deltaTime
			local newX = part.Position.X.Offset + speedX * deltaTime * 60
			local newY = part.Position.Y.Offset + speedY * deltaTime * 60
			part.Position = UDim2.new(0, newX, 0, newY)
			if tick() - createdTime > lifetime then
				connection:Disconnect()
				part:Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end
	task.delay(lifetime, function()
		if particlesFolder then
			particlesFolder:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end
local UIAspectRatioConstraint1 = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
UIAspectRatioConstraint1.Parent = discord_image
local discord_animation = false

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local freq = 2
local ampY = 0.009
local ampX = 0.023 
local random = Random.new()
local phaseX = random:NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2)
local phaseY = random:NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2)
basePos = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.45, 0)
TweenService:Create(discord_image, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = basePos}):Play()
game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if not discord_animation then return end
	local t = tick() * freq
	local offsetX = math.sin(t + phaseX) * ampX
	local offsetY = math.cos(t * 1.1 + phaseY) * ampY
	discord_image.Position = UDim2.new(
		basePos.X.Scale + offsetX, basePos.X.Offset,
		basePos.Y.Scale + offsetY, basePos.Y.Offset
	)
end)
